for all
b_{i},b_{j}\in{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g})^{\rm tw}
, which follow by a lengthy but direct computation.

7.5
A proof of relations (
5.11
) and (
5.12
)

Recall the horrific degree-2 and degree-3 relations of the twisted Yangian
{\mathcal{Y}}\big(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\theta}\big)^{\rm tw}
from the Theorem
5.5
:

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{q})]+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}(\bar{\mathfrak{c}}_{(\alpha)})^{-1}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w_{\alpha}^{\;rs}[\mathcal{B}(Y_{r}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{s})]=\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\lambda,\mu,\nu}\Lambda_{pq}^{\lambda\mu\nu}\{X_{\lambda},X_{\mu},X_{\nu}\},

\displaystyle[[\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{q})],\mathcal{B}(Y_{r})]+2\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}(\kappa_{\mathfrak{m}})^{tu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{r\alpha}^{\;\;\,s}[[\mathcal{B}(Y_{s}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{t})],\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})]

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\lambda,\mu,u}\Upsilon_{pqr}^{\lambda\mu u}\{X_{\lambda},X_{\mu},\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})\},

where, by (
5.13
) and (
5.14
),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{pq}^{\lambda\mu\nu}=\tfrac{1}{3}\Big(g^{\mu t}_{\;\;\,p}g^{\lambda u}_{\;\;\,q}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}(\bar{\mathfrak{c}}_{(\alpha)})^{-1}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w_{\alpha}^{\;rs}g^{\mu t}_{\;\;\,r}g^{\lambda u}_{\;\;\,s}\Big)w_{tu}^{\;\;\,\nu},

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{pqr}^{\lambda\mu u}=\tfrac{1}{4}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}\Big(w_{st}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{p}^{\;\lambda s}g_{q}^{\;\mu t}g_{\alpha r}^{\;\;\,u}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\beta}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}f_{\alpha}^{\;\lambda\beta}g_{r}^{\;\mu s}g_{\beta s}^{\;\;\,u}\Big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{\Upsilon_{pqr}^{\lambda\mu u} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\gamma}(\kappa_{\mathfrak{m}})^{vx}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\gamma}g_{r\gamma}^{\;\;\,y}\Big(w_{st}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{y}^{\;\lambda s}g_{v}^{\;\mu t}g_{\alpha x}^{\;\;\,u}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\beta}w_{yv}^{\;\;\,\alpha}f_{\alpha}^{\;\lambda\beta}g_{x}^{\;\mu s}g_{\beta s}^{\;\;\,u}\Big).

The coideal structure is given by the co-action
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}\colon{\mathcal{Y}}\big(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\theta}\big)^{\rm tw}\to{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g})\otimes{\mathcal{Y}}\big(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\theta}\big)^{\rm tw}
defined in (
5.15
):

\displaystyle\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}(X_{\alpha})=X_{\alpha}\otimes 1+1\otimes X_{\alpha},\qquad\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}(\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}))=\varphi(\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}))\otimes 1+1\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{p})+\hbar[Y_{p}\otimes 1,\Omega_{X}],

where, by (
5.16
),
\varphi(\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}))={\mathcal{J}}(Y_{p})+\tfrac{1}{4}\hbar[Y_{p},C_{X}]
.

Denote the horrific degree-2 and degree-3 relations as

\displaystyle H^{(2)}_{pq}=\hbar^{2}\Lambda_{pq}^{\lambda\mu\nu}\{X_{\lambda},X_{\mu},X_{\nu}\}\qquad\text{and}\qquad H^{(3)}_{pqr}=\hbar^{2}\Upsilon_{pqr}^{\lambda\mu u}\{X_{\lambda},X_{\mu},\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})\},

respectively. We need to show that co-action
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}
extends to a homomorphism of algebras
{\mathcal{Y}}\big(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\theta}\big)^{\rm tw}\to{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g})\otimes{\mathcal{Y}}\big(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}^{\theta}\big)^{\rm tw}
. This can be checked by a direct computation. We will demonstrate this in several steps.

Degree-2
. Set
B_{pq}=[{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{p}),{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{q})]
. We have that

\displaystyle\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}(B_{pq})=\varphi(B_{pq})\otimes 1+1\otimes B_{pq}+\hbar[\varphi(\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}))\otimes 1+1\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),[Y_{q}\otimes 1,\Omega_{X}]]

\displaystyle\hphantom{\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_\hbar(B_{pq}) =}{}+\hbar[[Y_{p}\otimes 1,\Omega_{X}],\varphi(\mathcal{B}(Y_{q}))\otimes 1+1\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{q})]+\hbar^{2}[[Y_{p}\otimes 1,\Omega_{X}],[Y_{q}\otimes 1,\Omega_{X}]].

The
\hbar
-order terms in the right hand side of the equality above equate to

\displaystyle\hbar\Big[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{p})+\tfrac{1}{4}\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}(Y_{r}X_{\alpha}+X_{\alpha}Y_{r})\otimes 1+1\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}Y_{r}\otimes X_{\alpha}\Big]-(p\leftrightarrow q)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}w_{pr}^{\;\;\,\beta}{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha}+\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}g_{q}^{\;\mu t}g_{p\mu}^{\;\;\,s}Y_{t}\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{s})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{q}^{\;\beta s}\Big(\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma}w_{rs}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\{X_{\gamma},X_{\alpha}\}+g_{\alpha s}^{\;\;\,\,t}\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\Big)\otimes X_{\beta}-(p\leftrightarrow q),

where
(p\leftrightarrow q)
denote all the terms with indices
p
and
q
interchanged. In a similar way the
\hbar^{2}
-order term equates to

\displaystyle\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{q}^{\;\beta s}[Y_{r}\otimes X_{\alpha},Y_{s}\otimes X_{\beta}]

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha s}g_{p}^{\;\beta t}\big)\big(w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\alpha}\}+f_{\alpha\beta}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\{Y_{s},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\gamma}\big).

Using mixed Jacobi identities we write

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}\big(g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}w_{pr}^{\;\;\,\beta}-g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}w_{qr}^{\;\;\,\beta}\big){\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}\big(g_{q}^{\;\mu t}g_{p\mu}^{\;\;\,s}-g_{p}^{\;\mu t}g_{q\mu}^{\;\;\,s}\big)Y_{t}\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{s})=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}w_{\mu}^{\;st}Y_{t}\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{s})

and

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}\big(g_{\alpha s}^{\;\;\,\,t}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{q}^{\;\beta s}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}g_{p}^{\;\beta s}\big)\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\beta}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma}f_{\alpha\beta}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}g_{p}^{\;\beta t}\big)\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\gamma}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}\big(g_{\alpha s}^{\;\;\,\,t}g_{q}^{\;\beta s}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma}f_{\alpha\gamma}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{q}^{\;\gamma t}\big)-g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}\big(g_{\alpha s}^{\;\;\,\,t}g_{p}^{\;\beta s}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma}f_{\alpha\gamma}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{p}^{\;\gamma t}\big)\big)\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\beta}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{s}^{\;t\beta}g_{q\alpha}^{\;\;\,s}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}g_{s}^{\;t\beta}g_{p\alpha}^{\;\;\,s}\big)\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\beta}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}w_{\mu}^{\;rs}g_{s}^{\;t\alpha}\{Y_{r},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\alpha}.

Using the identity

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\big(w_{rs}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha r}g_{q}^{\;\beta s}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha r}g_{p}^{\;\beta s}\big)\{X_{\gamma},X_{\alpha}\}\otimes X_{\beta}+w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}-g_{q}^{\;\alpha s}g_{p}^{\;\beta t}\big)X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\alpha}\}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}+g_{p}^{\;\beta s}g_{q}^{\;\alpha t}\big)(\{X_{\gamma},X_{\alpha}\}\otimes X_{\beta}+X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\alpha}\})

and combing all the expressions above we arrive to the following result,

\displaystyle\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}(B_{pq})=\varphi(B_{pq})\otimes 1+1\otimes B_{pq}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\Big(w_{\mu}^{\;st}Y_{t}\otimes{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{s})+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha}+\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}w_{\mu}^{\;\,su}g_{u}^{\;t\alpha}\{Y_{s},Y_{t}\}\otimes X_{\alpha}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}+g_{p}^{\;\beta s}g_{q}^{\;\alpha t}\big)(\{X_{\gamma},X_{\alpha}\}\otimes X_{\beta}+X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\alpha}\})

(7.24)

and, by similar computations,

\displaystyle\varphi(B_{pq})=[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{p}),{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{q})]+\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\Big(w_{\mu}^{\;st}\{{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{s}),Y_{t}\}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}\{{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta}),X_{\alpha}\}\Big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{\varphi(B_{pq}) =}{}+\tfrac{1}{4}\hbar^{2}g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}[\{Y_{s},X_{\alpha}\},\{Y_{t},X_{\beta}\}].

In a similar way as it was done in the proof of Lemma
7.1
, the last line of (
7.24
) can be split as

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{6}\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma,\lambda,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\lambda\gamma}f_{\lambda}^{\;\beta\alpha}(\{X_{\gamma},X_{\beta}\}\otimes X_{\alpha}-2X_{\alpha}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\gamma}\})

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}(\langle X_{\alpha},X_{\beta},X_{\gamma}\rangle_{1}+\langle X_{\alpha},X_{\beta},X_{\gamma}\rangle_{2}).

(7.25)

Here we have used the same notation as in (
5.3
). The terms in the second line of (
7.24
) and in the first line of (
7.25
) do not contribute to
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}

\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}\big(H^{(2)}_{pq}\big)
, since
w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}+\sum_{\alpha}(\bar{\mathfrak{c}}_{(\alpha)})^{-1}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w^{\;rs}_{\alpha}w_{rs}^{\;\;\,\mu}=0
. What remains are the terms in the second line of (

) giving

\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\!\!\mbox{

(7.26)

By (

) we have

\displaystyle\qquad{}=3\big(\langle X_{\alpha},X_{\beta},X_{\gamma}\rangle_{1}+\langle X_{\alpha},X_{\beta},X_{\gamma}\rangle_{2}\big).

(7.27)

Comparing (
7.26
) with (
7.27
) gives (
5.13
), as required.

Degree-3
. Set
B_{pqr}=[B_{pq},{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{r})]
. We write

\displaystyle\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}

{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}(B_{pqr})=\varphi(B_{pqr})\otimes 1+1\otimes B_{pqr}+\hbar B_{pqr}^{(2)}+\hbar^{2}B_{pqr}^{(1)}+\hbar^{3}B_{pqr}^{(0)},

(7.28)

where
B_{pqr}^{(i)}
denote elements of degree-
i
. By consistency, the elements of degree-2 in
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}\big(H^{(3)}_{pqr}\big)
must vanish altogether, since they are of a linear order in
\hbar
. This can be shown explicitly. We have

\displaystyle B_{pqr}^{(2)}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}[[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{p}),{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{q})]\otimes 1+1\otimes[\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{q})],g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}Y_{t}\otimes X_{\alpha}]

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2)} =}{}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\Big[w_{\mu}^{\;st}Y_{t}\otimes{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{s})+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha},{\mathcal{J}}(X_{r})\otimes 1+1\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{r})\Big].

Denote this expression as
B_{pqr}^{(2)}=B_{pqr}^{(2,0)}+B_{pqr}^{(0,2)}+B_{pqr}^{(1,1)}
, where
B_{pqr}^{(i,j)}
represents elements of degree-
i
in the left tensor factor and of degree-
j
in the right tensor factor. Then

\displaystyle B_{pqr}^{(2,0)}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}\big(w_{pt}^{\;\;\,\beta}[{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta}),{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{q})]+w_{qt}^{\;\;\,\beta}[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{p}),{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})]\big)\otimes X_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2,0)} =}{}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}[{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta}),{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{r})]\otimes X_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2,0)} }{}=2\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\Big(g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}(w_{pt}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{\beta q}^{\;\;\,s}-w_{qt}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{\beta p}^{\;\;\,s})+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}g_{\beta r}^{\;\;\,s}\Big)\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}g_{s}^{\;\gamma u}[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u}),{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\gamma})]\otimes X_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2,0)} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar^{2}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\Big(g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}(w_{pt}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{q}^{\gamma u}-w_{qt}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{p}^{\gamma u})+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}g_{r}^{\gamma u}\Big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2,0)} =}{}\times\textstyle\sum\limits_{ijk}\mathcal{A}_{\beta u\gamma}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}\otimes X_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(2,0)} }{}=2\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{\beta r}^{\;\;\,\,t}g_{t}^{\;\alpha s}g_{s}^{\;\gamma u}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}[{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u}),{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\gamma})]\otimes X_{\alpha}+\mathcal{O}_{1}\big(\hbar^{2}\big),

(7.29)

where
\mathcal{O}_{1}(\hbar^{2})
is a short–hand notation for the terms in the third line, and we have used the mixed Jacobi identities and the Drinfeld terrific relation (
5.6
) in the form

\displaystyle[{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta}),{\mathcal{J}}(X_{q})]+{2}{\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}}g_{\beta q}^{\;\;\,\,s}g_{s}^{\;\gamma u}[{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\gamma}),{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})]=\tfrac{1}{2}\hbar^{2}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma}\textstyle\sum\limits_{ijk}g_{q}^{\;\gamma u}\beta_{\beta u\gamma}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}.

Here the sum
\sum_{i}x_{i}
spans all of the symmetric space basis,
x_{i}=\{Y_{p},X_{\alpha}\}
; the same applies to
x_{j}
and
x_{k}
. In a similar way we find

\displaystyle B_{pqr}^{(0,2)}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}Y_{t}\otimes\big(g_{p\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}[\mathcal{B}(Y_{u}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{q})]+g_{q\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}[\mathcal{B}(Y_{p}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})]\big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(0,2)} =}{}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w_{\alpha}^{\;st}Y_{t}\otimes[{\mathcal{B}}(Y_{s}),\mathcal{B}(X_{r})]

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(0,2)}}{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}\Big(g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}(g_{p\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}w_{uq}^{\;\;\,\beta}-g_{q\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}w_{up}^{\;\;\,\beta})+w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w_{\alpha}^{\;st}w_{sr}^{\;\;\,\beta}\Big)(\bar{\mathfrak{c}}_{(\beta)})^{-1}w_{\beta}^{\;vs}Y_{t}\otimes[\mathcal{B}(Y_{s}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{v})]

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(0,2)} =}{}+\hbar^{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\lambda,\mu,\nu}\Big(g_{r}^{\;\alpha t}(g_{p\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}\Lambda_{uq}^{\lambda\mu\nu}-g_{q\alpha}^{\;\;\,u}\Lambda_{up}^{\lambda\mu\nu})+w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}w_{\alpha}^{\;st}\Lambda_{sr}^{\lambda\mu\nu}\Big)Y_{t}\otimes\{X_{\lambda},X_{\mu},X_{\nu}\}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(0,2)}}{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{\alpha r}^{\;\;\,\;t}g_{t}^{\;\beta t}w_{\beta}^{\;vs}(\bar{\mathfrak{c}}_{(\beta)})^{-1}Y_{t}\otimes[\mathcal{B}(Y_{s}),\mathcal{B}(Y_{v})]+\mathcal{O}_{2}\big(\hbar^{2}\big),

(7.30)

where we have used the mixed Jacobi identities and (
5.11
). The notation for
\mathcal{O}_{2}(\hbar^{2})
is the same as before. The remaining elements give

\displaystyle B_{pqr}^{(1,1)}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu,\beta}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\big(w_{\mu}^{\;st}w_{tr}^{\;\;\,\beta}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}g_{\alpha r}^{\;\;\,s}\big){\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{s})

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,1)}}{}=\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{\alpha r}^{\;\;\,\,t}g_{t}^{\;s\beta}{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{s}).

(7.31)

Now it is easy to check that leading terms in (
7.29
)–(
7.31
) do not contribute to
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}\big(H_{pqr}^{(3)}\big)
, since (by (
2.10
))

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\beta}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{\beta r}^{\;\;\,\,t}+2\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}(\kappa_{\mathfrak{m}})^{uv}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\alpha}g_{r\alpha}^{\;\;\,\,s}w_{su}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{\beta v}^{\;\;\,\,t}=0.

(7.32)

Next step is to consider the degree-1 term in (
7.28
). We write
B_{pqr}^{(1)}=B_{pqr}^{(1,0)}+B_{pqr}^{(0,1)}
. Using (
7.24
), (

) and (

) we find

\displaystyle B_{pqr}^{(1,0)}=\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\Big(\!\textstyle\sum\limits_{\beta,\gamma}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}g_{r}^{\;\gamma s}[{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta})\otimes X_{\alpha},2Y_{s}\otimes X_{\gamma}+\{Y_{s},X_{\gamma}\}\otimes 1]

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+w_{\mu}^{\;su}g_{u}^{\;t\alpha}[\{Y_{s},Y_{t}\},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{r})]\otimes X_{\alpha}\Big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}g_{r}^{\;\gamma u}\Big(w_{\mu}^{\;st}[\{{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{s}),Y_{t}\},Y_{u}]+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}[\{{\mathcal{J}}(X_{\beta}),X_{\alpha}\},Y_{u}]\Big)\otimes X_{\gamma}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}w_{st}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}+g_{p}^{\;\beta s}g_{q}^{\;\alpha t}\big)[\{X_{\gamma},X_{\alpha}\}\otimes X_{\beta}+X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\beta},X_{\alpha}\},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{r})\otimes 1]

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)}}{}=\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma,\lambda,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}\Big(\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta}g_{r}^{\;\beta s}\big(2f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\lambda}f_{\alpha\beta}^{\;\;\,\gamma}+f_{\mu}^{\;\gamma\alpha}f_{\alpha\beta}^{\;\;\,\lambda}\big)+w_{tr}^{\;\;\,\lambda}\big(w_{\mu}^{\;tu}g_{u}^{\;s\gamma}+w_{\mu}^{\;su}g_{u}^{\;t\gamma}\big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+g_{r}^{\;\gamma u}\big(w_{\mu}^{\;ts}w_{tu}^{\;\;\,\lambda}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\lambda}g_{\alpha u}^{\;\;\,s}\big)\Big)\{Y_{s},J(X_{\lambda})\}\otimes X_{\gamma}

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma,\mu}\big(w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}f_{\mu}^{\;\alpha\beta}g_{r}^{\;\gamma s}g_{\beta s}^{\;\;\,u}+w_{st}^{\;\;\,\beta}g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\gamma t}g_{\beta r}^{\;\;\,u}\big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}\times(2{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\otimes\{X_{\alpha},X_{\gamma}\}+\{X_{\alpha},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\}\otimes X_{\gamma}+\{X_{\gamma},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\}\otimes X_{\alpha})

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(1,0)} =}{}+\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}w_{ts}^{\;\;\,\gamma}\big(w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\beta}w_{\beta}^{\;ut}g_{r}^{\;\alpha s}+g_{r\beta}^{\;\;\,u}\big(g_{p}^{\;\beta s}g_{q}^{\;\alpha t}+g_{p}^{\;\alpha s}g_{q}^{\;\beta t}\big)\big)\{X_{\gamma},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\}\otimes X_{\alpha}.

(7.33)

The first sum in the last equality can be simplified using the mixed Jacobi identities, giving

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\gamma,\lambda,\mu}w_{pq}^{\;\;\,\mu}g_{\mu r}^{\;\;\,t}\big(g_{t}^{\;u\lambda}g_{u}^{\gamma s}+\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha}g_{t}^{\;\alpha s}f_{\alpha}^{\gamma\lambda}\big)\{Y_{s},J(X_{\lambda})\}\otimes X_{\gamma}.

\displaystyle\hphantom{B_{pqr}^{(0,1)} =}{}\times\Big(X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\alpha},\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})\}+\tfrac{1}{2}\{X_{\alpha},X_{\gamma}\}\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})\Big).

). Set

. The contribution of the remaining sums in (

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\textstyle\sum\limits_{\alpha,\gamma}\Upsilon_{pqr}^{\alpha\gamma u}\big({\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\otimes\{X_{\alpha},X_{\gamma}\}+2\{X_{\alpha},{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\}\otimes X_{\gamma}\\

(7.35)

Using (

\displaystyle\qquad{}={\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u})\otimes\{X_{\alpha},X_{\gamma}\}+\{X_{\alpha},X_{\gamma}\}\otimes\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+(\{{\mathcal{J}}(Y_{u}),X_{\alpha}\}\otimes X_{\gamma}+X_{\gamma}\otimes\{X_{\alpha},\mathcal{B}(Y_{u})\}+(\alpha\leftrightarrow\gamma)),

where
\mathcal{O}_{3}(\hbar)
denotes linear in
\hbar
terms that appear due to (

). Comparing the relation above with (

) and using symmetry

gives (

), as required. The remaining elements in

, that we have denoted by

and, by summing with

of (

7.6
An outline of a proof of relations (
5.18
)–(
5.22
)

The following lemma, which follows by a direct computation, will be necessary in what follows below.

Lemma 7.2
.

In any associative algebra generated by elements
x_{a(b,c,\ldots)}
over
\mathbb{C}
the following identities hold:

\displaystyle\{x_{i},\{x_{j},x_{k}\}\}=\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}+\tfrac{1}{12}[x_{(j},[x_{k)},x_{i}]],

\displaystyle\{x_{j},x_{k},x_{l},x_{m}\}=\tfrac{1}{3}\{x_{j},x_{(k},\{x_{l},x_{m)}\}\}-\tfrac{1}{36}[[x_{j},x_{(k}],\{x_{l},x_{m)}\}],

\displaystyle\{1\otimes x_{i},1\otimes x_{j},x_{a}\otimes\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\}=x_{a}\otimes\{x_{i},x_{j},\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\},

\displaystyle\{x_{i}\otimes 1,1\otimes x_{j},x_{a}\otimes\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\}=\{x_{i},x_{a}\}\otimes\{x_{j},\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\}-\tfrac{1}{12}[x_{a},x_{i}]\otimes[x_{j},\{x_{b},x_{c}\}],

\displaystyle\{x_{i}\otimes 1,1\otimes x_{j},\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\otimes x_{a}\}=\{x_{i},\{x_{b},x_{c}\}\}\otimes\{x_{j},x_{a}\}-\tfrac{1}{12}[\{x_{b},x_{c}\},x_{i}]\otimes[x_{j},x_{a}].

Recall the horrific degree-4 relation (
5.18
) of the twisted Yangian
{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g})^{\rm tw}
in Theorem
5.6
:

\displaystyle[\mathcal{G}(x_{a}),\mathcal{G}([x_{b},x_{c}])]+[\mathcal{G}(x_{b}),\mathcal{G}([x_{c},x_{a}])]+[\mathcal{G}(x_{c}),\mathcal{G}([x_{a},x_{b}])]=\hbar^{2}\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},\mathcal{G}(x_{k})\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\hbar^{4}(\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}+\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{ijklm}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k},x_{l},x_{m}\}),

where, by (
5.19
)–(
5.21
),

\displaystyle\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}=\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}\alpha_{c)r}^{\hskip 8.19447ptk}\overline{h}_{d}^{rij}-\alpha_{dr}^{\;\;\,k}\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}\overline{h}_{c)}^{rij},\qquad\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{\,ijklm}=\tfrac{1}{5}\big(\alpha_{rs}^{\;\;\,i}\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}h_{c)}^{rjk}h_{d}^{slm}-\Psi_{abc}^{jkr}h_{r}^{ilm}\big),

\displaystyle\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}=\tfrac{1}{9}\Big(\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}W_{c)d}^{ijk}+\tfrac{1}{6}\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{(ix(yzj))}\alpha_{xy}^{\;\;\,r}\alpha_{rz}^{\;\;\,k}-\big(\Psi_{abc}^{xjy}\overline{h}_{y}^{kzr}\alpha_{zx}^{\;\;\,s}\alpha_{rs}^{\;\;\,i}+\Psi_{abc}^{xyz}h_{z}^{rsk}\alpha_{rx}^{\;\;\,i}\alpha_{ys}^{\;\;\,j}\big)\Big).

with

\displaystyle W_{cd}^{ijk}=\alpha_{rs}^{\;\;\,i}h_{c}^{rxy}\big(h_{d}^{szk}\alpha_{xt}^{\;\;\,j}\alpha_{yz}^{\;\;\,t}+h_{d}^{szt}\alpha_{xt}^{\;\;\,k}\alpha_{yz}^{\;\;\,j}\big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{W_{cd}^{ijk} =}{}+\Big(\big(\overline{h}_{c}^{xyz}h_{d}^{efk}-\overline{h}_{d}^{xyz}h_{c}^{efk}\big)\alpha_{ye}^{\;\;\,t}\alpha_{zt}^{\;\;\,i}\alpha_{xf}^{\;\;\,j}+\overline{h}_{c}^{jxy}\overline{h}_{d}^{kzr}\alpha_{xr}^{\;\;\,s}\big(\alpha_{zy}^{\;\;\,t}\alpha_{st}^{\;\;\,i}+\alpha_{sy}^{\;\;\,t}\alpha_{zt}^{\;\;\,i})\Big)

and

\displaystyle h_{a}^{\;bcd}=\phi_{a}^{\;bcd}+2\psi_{a}^{\;bcd},\qquad\overline{h}_{a}^{\;bcd}=\phi_{a}^{\;bcd}-\psi_{a}^{\;bcd},\qquad\psi_{a}^{\;bcd}=\tfrac{1}{12}\big(\alpha_{a}^{\;jd}\alpha_{j}^{\;bc}+\alpha_{a}^{\;jc}\alpha_{j}^{\;bd}\big),

\displaystyle\phi_{a}^{\;bcd}=\tfrac{1}{24}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathfrak{g}}^{-1}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\pi}\big(\alpha_{a}^{\;jk}\alpha_{j}^{\;\pi(d)r}\alpha_{k}^{\;\pi(b)s}\alpha_{sr}^{\;\;\,\pi(c)}\big).

Denote the degree-4 relation as

\displaystyle H^{(4)}_{abc}=\hbar^{2}\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},\mathcal{G}(x_{k})\}+\hbar^{4}\big(\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}+\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{ijklm}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k},x_{l},x_{m}\}\big).

(7.36)

The coefficients
\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}
,
\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}
,
\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{ijklm}\in\mathbb{C}
must be designed to make
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -6.4 L 7.87 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\hskip 0.7113pt_{\hbar}
extend to a homomorphism of algebras
{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g})^{\rm tw}\to{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g})\otimes{\mathcal{Y}}(\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g})^{\rm tw}
. We will find these coefficients by acting with
\!\!\mbox{
\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-5.02351pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{}{}

and comparing the elements on the both sides of the resulting equality we find

\displaystyle\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}=\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}\alpha_{c)r}^{\hskip 8.19447ptk}\overline{h}_{d}^{rij}-\alpha_{dr}^{\;\;\,k}\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}\overline{h}_{c)}^{rij},

\displaystyle\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{(ij(klm))}=3\big(\alpha_{rs}^{\;\;\,i}\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}h_{c)}^{rjk}h_{d}^{slm}-\Psi_{abc}^{jkr}h_{r}^{ilm}\big),

\displaystyle\Phi_{abc}^{(ijk)}=\tfrac{1}{3}\alpha_{(ab}^{\hskip 8.19447ptd}W_{c)d}^{ijk}+\tfrac{1}{18}\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{(ix(yzj))}\alpha_{xy}^{\;\;\,r}\alpha_{rz}^{\;\;\,k}-\tfrac{1}{3}\big(\Psi_{abc}^{xjy}\overline{h}_{y}^{kzr}\alpha_{zx}^{\;\;\,s}\alpha_{rs}^{\;\;\,i}+\Psi_{abc}^{xyz}h_{z}^{rsk}\alpha_{rx}^{\;\;\,i}\alpha_{ys}^{\;\;\,j}\big),

where in the last expression we used the symmetries
\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}=\Psi_{abc}^{jik}
and
\Phi_{abc}^{(ij(klm))}=\Phi_{abc}^{(ij(kml))}
. Finally, using

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{3}\Phi_{abc}^{(ijk)}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\}=\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k}\},

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{15}\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{(ij(klm))}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k},x_{l},x_{m}\}=\Phi_{abc}^{ijklm}\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{k},x_{l},x_{m}\},

we obtain (
5.19
), (
5.20
) and (
5.21
). To complete the proof it would require to compute all elements in

as we did in Section
7.5
above. Here we will not attempt to reach this goal. An important question is whether the coefficients
\Psi_{abc}^{ijk}
,
\Phi_{abc}^{ijk}
and
\overline{\Phi}_{abc}^{\,ijklm}
can be written in an elegant and compact form. We leave these goals as open questions for a further study.
